682:#  passed the 85-row version -- `warm once' interleaves only 8-byte accesses; the Opus seat measured it 85/85).  One
683:#  refusal latches; then each ACCEPTED kind -- 8W, 8R, 4W and 4R in both lanes, and an accepted access into each switch
684:#  arm (DOG, TIME, INTMASK, INTSTAT, REV, CONTROL, the default arm low and in the far block) -- is followed by a refusal.
685:#  A re-arm anywhere an accepted access reaches shows as a second notice.  (A re-arm in the common tail, which refusals
686:#  also reach, shows in latch x5.)
687:#  The second closing pass (codex) found the arm's BRANCHES were not all reached: an accepted CONTROL write carried only
688:#  0, 1 or 0x800, so a re-arm inside the DOG_ENABLE branch passed.  So the CONTROL write here is 0x0201 (the DOG branch
689:#  and the unimplemented-control branch), the REV read runs from the PROM-probe window (its pc-redirect branch), and the
690:#  default arm takes a 4-byte write too.  The HARD_RESET branch cannot be followed by anything (it stops every CPU):
691:#  a re-arm there is DISCLOSED.
692:A = Addr(CODE)
693:c = ["ra=0x%x" % RA]
694:x, _ = ld(A, "lb", CRIME + 0x07, "v0"); c += x
695:#  The THIRD closing pass (the Opus seat, measured) found the list still built from the members earlier passes had
696:#  found rather than from the function: a CONTROL READ, a CONTROL write with DOG clear, and one whose leftover bits are
697:#  zero each re-armed at 121/121.  So the kinds are now DERIVED FROM DEVICE_ACCESS(crime)'s OWN BRANCHES -- both sides
698:#  of every `if' an accepted access can take and come back from: len 8 (W, R), len 4 (W, R) x (relative_addr & 4),
699:#  every case label, the REV pc-window test (in, out), CONTROL's writeflag test (R, W) x its DOG test (set, clear) x
700:#  its leftover test (nonzero, zero), the default arm (R, W).  HARD_RESET's true side stops the CPUs: disclosed.
701:#  *** And the FOURTH closing pass (the Opus seat, measured; kimi CLI, deepseek by reading) found that list had each
702:#  branch's two sides SOMEWHERE -- the marginals -- but not the KIND crossed with the LABEL the contract promises: a
703:#  re-arm on an accepted WRITE at INTSTAT/INTMASK or REV, a pre-set on a write at +0x10, and CONTROL's (DOG set,
704:#  leftover 0) cell (0x0200) each scored 131/131 while changing the announce count.  So the list is now the CROSS:
705:#  {8W, 8R, 4W, 4R} at every label (a label is 8-aligned, so its 4-byte access is the high lane), REV also from the
706:#  PROM-probe window in both directions (the pc test ignores the direction), CONTROL's writes at both widths x
707:#  {0x0201, 0x0001, 0, 0x0200}, and the default arm's six kinds in both 4-byte lanes.  ONE list, used here (re-arm)
708:#  AND as latch x5's prologue (pre-set). ***
709:ACCEPTS = (("sd", 0x00, 0), ("ld", 0x00, 0), ("sw", 0x00, 0), ("lw", 0x00, 0),
710:           ("lwP", 0x00, 0), ("swP", 0x00, 0), ("sdP", 0x00, 0),
711:           ("lw", 0x08, 0), ("ld", 0x08, 0),
712:           ("sw", 0x08, 0x0201), ("sw", 0x08, 0x0001), ("sw", 0x08, 0), ("sw", 0x08, 0x0200),
713:           ("sd", 0x08, 0x0201), ("sd", 0x08, 0x0001), ("sd", 0x08, 0), ("sd", 0x08, 0x0200),
714:           ("sd", 0x10, 0), ("ld", 0x10, 0), ("sw", 0x10, 0), ("lw", 0x10, 0),
715:           ("sd", 0x18, 0), ("ld", 0x18, 0), ("sw", 0x18, 0), ("lw", 0x18, 0),
716:           ("sd", 0x30, 0), ("ld", 0x30, 0), ("sw", 0x30, 0), ("lw", 0x30, 0),
717:           ("sd", 0x38, 0), ("ld", 0x38, 0), ("sw", 0x38, 0), ("lw", 0x38, 0),
718:           ("sd", 0x40, 0), ("ld", 0x40, 0), ("sw", 0x40, 0), ("lw", 0x40, 0),
719:           ("sw", 0x34, 0x0badcafe), ("lw", 0x34, 0), ("sw", 0x04, 0), ("lw", 0x04, 0),
720:           ("sd", 0x278, 0), ("ld", 0x278, 0))
721:KINDS = ACCEPTS
722:
723:
724:def accept(A, op, off, val, reg="a0"):
725:    """one ACCEPTED access of ACCEPTS; the `P' kinds run from pc 0xbfc05180, the REV arm's redirect window (pc = ra)"""
726:    if op.endswith("P"):
727:        if op[0] == "s":
728:            x, _ = st(A, op[:2], CRIME + off, val, at=PROBE_PC)
729:        else:
730:            x, _ = ld(A, op[:2], CRIME + off, reg, at=PROBE_PC)
731:    elif op[0] == "s":
732:        x, _ = st(A, op, CRIME + off, val)
733:    else:
734:        x, _ = ld(A, op, CRIME + off, reg)
735:    return x
736:
737:
738:for op, off, val in KINDS:
739:    c += accept(A, op, off, val)
740:    x, _ = ld(A, "lb", CRIME + 0x06, "v0"); c += x
741:t, clean = session(c + roundtrip(A))
742:row("rearm once", count(t, "-- declined.", clean), 1, "%d refusals, %d accepted kinds between: one notice"
743:    % (len(KINDS) + 1, len(KINDS)))
744:row("rearm surv", pr(t, "v1"), "0x%x" % LIVE_VAL, "the whole interleave completes (a RAM trip)")
745:
746:# ---- ACCEPT cells at the window's LAST 8-byte slot (+0x278; no case label, no prelude write) -- the far bound ----
747:A = Addr(CODE)
748:c = []
749:x, _ = ld(A, "ld", CRIME + 0x210, "s2"); c += x
750:x, _ = ld(A, "lw", CRIME + 0x214, "s1"); c += x      # 4R of the anchored slot's low half
751:#  4R of its HIGH half (the Opus closing seat: `len == 4 && relative_addr != 0x210' passed the 85-row version).  Its
752:#  value is 0 accepted or refused -- the prelude rewrites the slot on every access -- so the row is the ANNOUNCE: a
753:#  refused accept cell here takes the session's one notice, and `top said' below reads 0.
754:x, _ = ld(A, "lw", CRIME + 0x210, "t1"); c += x
755:x, _ = st(A, "sd", CRIME + 0x278, 0x0102030405060708); c += x
756:x, _ = ld(A, "lb", CRIME + 0x27b, "v0"); c += x      # a REFUSED read while the far slot is nonzero (Opus: rd_hi)
757:x, _ = ld(A, "lh", CRIME + 0x27a, "s3"); c += x      # and a refused 2-byte read there (Opus, second closing pass)
758:x, _ = ld(A, "ld", CRIME + 0x278, "a0"); c += x
759:x, _ = ld(A, "lw", CRIME + 0x278, "a1"); c += x
760:x, _ = ld(A, "lw", CRIME + 0x27c, "a2"); c += x
761:x, _ = st(A, "sw", CRIME + 0x27c, 0x0a0b0c0d); c += x
762:x, _ = st(A, "sw", CRIME + 0x278, 0x11112222); c += x
763:x, _ = ld(A, "ld", CRIME + 0x278, "s0"); c += x
764:t, clean = session(c + ["print s2", "print a0", "print a1", "print a2", "print s0", "print s1", "print v0",
765:                        "print s3"])
766:row("top8 anchor", pr(t, "s2"), "0x1", "8R +0x210: bank-1 ctrl the PRELUDE wrote (-M 64)")
767:row("top4 anchor", pr(t, "s1"), "0x1", "4R +0x214: the anchored slot's low half")
768:row("top rd v0", pr(t, "v0"), "0x0", "refused lb +0x27b over a nonzero slot answers 0")
769:row("top rd2 v0", pr(t, "s3"), "0x0", "refused lh +0x27a over a nonzero slot answers 0")
770:row("top said", said(t, 1, 0x27b, clean), 1, "lb +0x27b is the first refusal: every access before it accepted")
771:row("top8 st+ld", pr(t, "a0"), "0x102030405060708", "8W then 8R at +0x278")
772:row("top4 rhi", pr(t, "a1"), "0x1020304", "4R high half at +0x278")
773:row("top4 rlo", pr(t, "a2"), "0x5060708", "4R low half at +0x27c")
774:row("top4 w", pr(t, "s0"), "0x111122220a0b0c0d", "4W both halves at +0x278/+0x27c")
775:
776:# ---- a refused width at the window's LAST byte: declined, announced, default arm not reached ----
777:#  The far slot is SEEDED first (codex, third closing pass: a refused store that CLEARS -- `&= idata' -- left the unseeded
778:#  slot at 0, which `hi keeps' wanted).
779:A = Addr(CODE)
780:sf, _ = st(A, "sd", CRIME + 0x278, 0x0123456789abcdef)
781:x, here = st(A, "sb", CRIME + 0x27f, 0xff)
782:w, _ = st(A, "sh", CRIME + 0x27c, 0xffff)      # a refused 2-byte store in the far block too (Opus closing: wst_mc2)
783:y, _ = ld(A, "ld", CRIME + 0x278, "a0")        # the refused stores' register, read back by an ACCEPTED ld
784:z, _ = st(A, "sb", CRIME + 0x27e, 0xee)        # follow-up refusals: one in the high block (a latch keyed on >= 0x40)
785:z2, _ = ld(A, "lb", CRIME + 0x07, "v0")        #   and one low (a latch whose bit depends on the block, `lt_blk')
786:t, clean = session(sf + x + AFTER + roundtrip(A) + w + y + ["print a0"] + z + z2)
787:row("hi said", said(t, 1, 0x27f, clean), 1, "sb +0x27f: own announce, once")
788:row("hi nodflt", dflt(t, clean, 0x27f, True), 0, "switch default arm NOT reached at 0x27f")
789:row("hi surv", pr(t, "v1"), "0x%x" % LIVE_VAL, "host survives, then a RAM trip")
790:row("hi keeps", pr(t, "a0"), "0x123456789abcdef", "refused sb +0x27f, sh +0x27c leave the seeded slot (ld +0x278)")
791:row("hi once", count(t, "-- declined.", clean), 1, "first refusal +0x27f, then 0x27c/0x27e/0x07: one notice")
792:
793:# ---- THE MIDDLE OF THE WINDOW, where EVERY address bit 0-8 is set (kimi-k3:cloud, second closing pass: no refused access
794:#  set bit 7 or bit 8, so a decline effect keyed on `relative_addr & 0x80' -- `odata = relative_addr & 0x80' -- passed).
795:#  Together with the low cells (bits clear) and the far block (bit 9), every address bit is now carried both ways by a
796:#  refused access of each kind: the ANSWER (1R, 2R), a LANDING (1W, 2W, read back by an accepted ld), the NOTICE (its
797:#  text here, and one count across the session), the DEFAULT ARM not reached, and a RE-ARM by an accepted access in
798:#  this block (the sd between the refusals).  +0x1f8 is inside DEV_CRIME_LENGTH (0x280) and reaches the default arm.
799:A = Addr(CODE)
800:c = []
801:x, _ = st(A, "sd", CRIME + 0x1f8, 0x0123456789abcdef); c += x
802:x, _ = ld(A, "lb", CRIME + 0x1ff, "v0"); c += x
803:x, _ = ld(A, "lh", CRIME + 0x1fe, "a1"); c += x
804:x, _ = st(A, "sb", CRIME + 0x1ff, 0xff); c += x
805:x, _ = st(A, "sh", CRIME + 0x1fc, 0xffff); c += x

import ast, pathlib, re, collections
p = pathlib.Path(r'C:\\DocumentNoSnc\\CC\\GXEMUL\\GXEMUL-SEC\\regress\\crime_width_probe.py')
tree = ast.parse(p.read_text())
sessions = []
def capture_session(cmds, **kwargs):
    sessions.append({'cmds': list(cmds), 'rows': [], 'accesses': []})
    return '', True
def capture_row(name, got, want, note=''):
    sessions[-1]['rows'].append(name)
body = [n for n in tree.body if 213 <= n.lineno <= 927 and not (isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name in ('session', 'row'))]
ns = {'re': re, 'session': capture_session, 'row': capture_row, 'print': lambda *args, **kwargs: None}
exec(compile(ast.Module(body=body, type_ignores=[]), str(p) + ':COMMAND_GENERATION_ONLY', 'exec'), ns)
op_names = {v: k for k, v in ns['OPC'].items()}
reg_names = {v: k for k, v in ns['R'].items()}
widths = {'lb':1,'lbu':1,'sb':1,'lh':2,'lhu':2,'sh':2,'lw':4,'sw':4,'ld':8,'sd':8}
for s in sessions:
    regs, code, pc = {}, {}, None
    for cmd in s['cmds']:
        m = re.fullmatch(r'put w (0x[0-9a-f]+), (0x[0-9a-f]+)', cmd)
        if m:
            code[int(m[1],16)] = int(m[2],16)
        elif '=' in cmd:
            k, v = cmd.split('=',1)
            if k == 'pc': pc = int(v,16)
            else: regs[k] = int(v,16)
        elif cmd.startswith('step ') or cmd == '<RUN>':
            ins = code[pc]
            op = op_names.get(ins >> 26)
            if op:
                rs = reg_names[(ins >> 21) & 31]
                rt = reg_names[(ins >> 16) & 31]
                addr = regs[rs] + (ins & 0xffff)
                if ns['CRIME'] <= addr < ns['CRIME'] + 0x280:
                    s['accesses'].append((op, addr-ns['CRIME'], pc, regs.get(rt)))
acc = ns['ACCEPTS']
print('COMMAND GENERATION ONLY: no emulator, process launch, sleep, or file write')
print('ACCEPTS tuples:',len(acc),'generated sessions:',len(sessions),'generated rows:',sum(len(s['rows']) for s in sessions))
print('REV PROM kinds:',[x for x in acc if x[0].endswith('P')])
print('CRIME_REV accesses in PROM window:',dict(collections.Counter(op for s in sessions for op,off,pc,val in s['accesses'] if off == 0 and (pc & 0xffffff00) == 0xbfc05100)))
for name in ('rearm once','latch x5'):
    s = next(s for s in sessions if name in s['rows'])
    hits = [x for x in s['accesses'] if x[0] == 'ld' and x[1] == 0 and (x[2] & 0xffffff00) == 0xbfc05100]
    print(name, 'targeted 8R-in-PROM hits:',len(hits),'accesses:',len(s['accesses']))
print('All sessions targeted 8R-in-PROM hits:',sum(1 for s in sessions for op,off,pc,val in s['accesses'] if op == 'ld' and off == 0 and (pc & 0xffffff00) == 0xbfc05100))
def kind(x):
    op, off, val = x
    if off == 0: return ('REV',op[:2],op.endswith('P'))
    if off == 8: return ('CONTROL',op,bool(val & 0x200),bool(val & ~(0x200|0x800|0x100)))
    if off in (0x10,0x18,0x30,0x38): return (hex(off),op)
    return ('default',op, bool(off & 4) if op in ('lw','sw') else None)
counts=collections.Counter(kind(x) for x in acc)
print('Distinct returning access-path classes under the brief scope:',len(counts))
print('Repeated classes:',[(k,n) for k,n in counts.items() if n>1])

"C:\\WINDOWS\\System32\\WindowsPowerShell\\v1.0\\powershell.exe" -Command "@'
import ast, pathlib, re, collections
p = pathlib.Path(r'C:\\DocumentNoSnc\\CC\\GXEMUL\\GXEMUL-SEC\\regress\\crime_width_probe.py')
tree = ast.parse(p.read_text())
sessions = []
def capture_session(cmds, **kwargs):
    sessions.append({'cmds': list(cmds), 'rows': [], 'accesses': []})
    return '', True
def capture_row(name, got, want, note=''):
    sessions[-1]['rows'].append(name)
body = [n for n in tree.body if 213 <= n.lineno <= 927 and not (isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name in ('session', 'row'))]
ns = {'re': re, 'BIN':'UNUSED', 'LABEL':'COMMANDS_ONLY', 'session': capture_session, 'row': capture_row, 'print': lambda *args, **kwargs: None}
exec(compile(ast.Module(body=body, type_ignores=[]), str(p) + ':COMMAND_GENERATION_ONLY', 'exec'), ns)
op_names = {v: k for k, v in ns['OPC'].items()}
reg_names = {v: k for k, v in ns['R'].items()}
widths = {'lb':1,'lbu':1,'sb':1,'lh':2,'lhu':2,'sh':2,'lw':4,'sw':4,'ld':8,'sd':8}
for s in sessions:
    regs, code, pc = {}, {}, None
    for cmd in s['cmds']:
        m = re.fullmatch(r'put w (0x[0-9a-f]+), (0x[0-9a-f]+)', cmd)
        if m:
            code[int(m[1],16)] = int(m[2],16)
        elif '=' in cmd:
            k, v = cmd.split('=',1)
            if k == 'pc': pc = int(v,16)
            else: regs[k] = int(v,16)
        elif cmd.startswith('step ') or cmd == '<RUN>':
            ins = code[pc]
            op = op_names.get(ins >> 26)
            if op:
                rs = reg_names[(ins >> 21) & 31]
                rt = reg_names[(ins >> 16) & 31]
                addr = regs[rs] + (ins & 0xffff)
                if ns['CRIME'] <= addr < ns['CRIME'] + 0x280:
                    s['accesses'].append((op, addr-ns['CRIME'], pc, regs.get(rt)))
acc = ns['ACCEPTS']
print('COMMAND GENERATION ONLY: no emulator, process launch, sleep, or file write')
print('ACCEPTS tuples:',len(acc),'generated sessions:',len(sessions),'generated rows:',sum(len(s['rows']) for s in sessions))
print('REV PROM kinds:',[x for x in acc if x[0].endswith('P')])
print('CRIME_REV accesses in PROM window:',dict(collections.Counter(op for s in sessions for op,off,pc,val in s['accesses'] if off == 0 and (pc & 0xffffff00) == 0xbfc05100)))
for name in ('rearm once','latch x5'):
    s = next(s for s in sessions if name in s['rows'])
    hits = [x for x in s['accesses'] if x[0] == 'ld' and x[1] == 0 and (x[2] & 0xffffff00) == 0xbfc05100]
    print(name, 'targeted 8R-in-PROM hits:',len(hits),'accesses:',len(s['accesses']))
print('All sessions targeted 8R-in-PROM hits:',sum(1 for s in sessions for op,off,pc,val in s['accesses'] if op == 'ld' and off == 0 and (pc & 0xffffff00) == 0xbfc05100))
def kind(x):
    op, off, val = x
    if off == 0: return ('REV',op[:2],op.endswith('P'))
    if off == 8: return ('CONTROL',op,bool(val & 0x200),bool(val & ~(0x200|0x800|0x100)))
    if off in (0x10,0x18,0x30,0x38): return (hex(off),op)
    return ('default',op, bool(off & 4) if op in ('lw','sw') else None)
counts=collections.Counter(kind(x) for x in acc)
print('Distinct returning access-path classes under the brief scope:',len(counts))
print('Repeated classes:',[(k,n) for k,n in counts.items() if n>1])

def announce_count(accesses, mutation):
    warned, notices = False, 0
    for op, off, pc, val in accesses:
        if widths[op] not in (4,8):
            if not warned:
                notices += 1
                warned = True
        elif op == 'ld' and off == 0 and (pc & 0xffffff00) == 0xbfc05100:
            if mutation == 'rearm': warned = False
            elif mutation == 'preset': warned = True
    return notices
print('Latch-only model, not emulator measurements:')
for nm, mut in [('rearm once','rearm'),('latch x5','preset')]:
    s = next(s for s in sessions if nm in s['rows'])
    seq = s['accesses']
    if mut == 'rearm':
        extended = seq[:1] + [('ld',0,ns['PROBE_PC'],None)] + seq[1:]
    else:
        extended = [('ld',0,ns['PROBE_PC'],None)] + seq
    print(nm,'current base/mutant:',announce_count(seq,'base'),announce_count(seq,mut),
          'with ldP base/mutant:',announce_count(extended,'base'),announce_count(extended,mut))

`CRIME_CLOSE5_20260914`

1. **`ACCEPTS` omits `("ldP", 0x00, 0)`.** Its PROM-window entries are `lwP`, `swP`, and `sdP`; the function also accepts an 8-byte read there. See [the enumeration](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/crime_width_probe.py:709) and [the REV branch](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_sgi_ip32.c:344).

   I found no structurally impossible listed tuple. The represented cells have usable notice-count channels for their respective pre-set/re-arm mutations. The missing cell reaches neither channel: expanding the command generation for all **41 sessions and 141 rows** found **zero** `ld` accesses to REV within the PROM window.

2. **The exact edit above is a predicted 141/141 survivor.** `rearm once` should catch it, but never executes its trigger. The other sessions do not execute it either.

   It is non-equivalent: refuse once, execute an accepted `ld` at REV from the PROM window with a valid `ra`, then refuse again. The mutant announces twice; the fix announces once. Replacing the inserted assignment with `d->warned |= CRIME_W_LEN;` produces the corresponding pre-set gap: `latch x5` never exercises that trigger.

   This belongs to the expressly promised accepted-kind cross. The disclosed limitation on joint refusal predicates does not close this omission.

   Add `("ldP", 0x00, 0)` to the shared list. That gives 44 entries while retaining 141 rows. The latch model predicts:
   
   - Re-arm mutant: `rearm once` changes from **1 to 2**, failing.
   - Pre-set mutant: `latch x5` changes from **1 to 0**, failing.

3. **The completeness statements are wrong or overbroad.** “Repair 10 … closes the cross” and items 4/8’s complete-cross wording fail on `ldP`. Table 8’s recorded kills do not establish that universal claim: [its `x_rearm_rev8r` mutation](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/crimewidthkill/killtable8_20260914/logs/x_rearm_rev8r.diff) sits **outside** the PC-window condition, so an ordinary REV `ld` kills it.

   Item 9’s statement that “every lvalue the function reads is nonzero where a refused answer is read” is independently false. `d->use_fb` remains zero in this configuration, consistent with the required REV value `0x11`. Inserting `odata = d->use_fb;` immediately before the decline’s `goto` is another predicted survivor under that literal contract; with a nonzero framebuffer flag, refused reads would answer nonzero. That configuration limitation needs explicit disclosure or coverage.

I verified the shipped and frozen Table 8 probes have identical hashes. These new survivor scores are **static predictions, not emulator measurements**; this workspace is read-only. The missing measurements are isolated `ldP` re-arm and pre-set mutants against the original and amended list, to confirm the predicted count changes.
